Convert full-string date\n    m = re.fullmatch(r\"(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})\", s.strip())\n    if not m:\n        return s\n    y, mo, d = m.groups()\n    return f\"{y}.{mo}.{d}\"\n\n\ndef convert_dates_in_text(text: str) -> str:\n    return DATE_RE.sub(lambda m: f\"{m.group(1)}.{m.group(2)}.{m.group(3)}\", text)\n\n\ndef risk_with_note(risk: str) -> str:\n    risk = (risk or \"\").strip()\n    note = RISK_NOTE.get(risk)\n    if not note:\n        # Unknown tier: keep as-is (but still ensure no trailing/leading space)\n        return risk\n    return f\"{risk} ({note})\"\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    # '{uri}name' -> 'name'\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef build_replacements(overview: dict, actions: dict) -> dict[str, str]:\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\", {}) if isinstance(overview, dict) else {}\n\n    # Base replacements from overview.summary keys\n    repl: dict[str, str] = {}\n    if isinstance(summary, dict):\n        for k, v in summary.items():\n            if v is None:\n                continue\n            if isinstance(v, (dict, list)):\n                # Not expected in this task; stringify conservatively\n                repl[k] = json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)\n            else:\n                repl[k] = str(v)\n\n    # Date conversion for the inspection date field if present\n    if \"점검일\" in repl:\n        repl[\"점검일\"] = to_dot_date(repl[\"점검일\"])\n\n    # Risk tier mapping\n    if \"위험등급\" in repl:\n        repl[\"위험등급\"] = risk_with_note(repl[\"위험등급\"])\n\n    # Corrective actions: fill in order as provided\n    immediate = []\n    if isinstance(actions, dict):\n        immediate = actions.get(\"immediate_actions\", [])\n    if not isinstance(immediate, list):\n        immediate = []\n    immediate_strs = [str(x) for x in immediate]\n\n    for i in range(3):\n        key = f\"조치{i+1}\"\n        repl[key] = immediate_strs[i] if i < len(immediate_strs) else \"\"\n\n    # Parent map for removals of nested elements\n    parent_map = {child: parent for parent in p_el.iter() for child in parent}\n\n    remove_names = {\n        \"linesegarray\",\n        \"lineSegArray\",\n        \"linesegArray\",\n        \"cache\",\n        \"parameterSet\",\n        \"parameterset\",\n    }\n\n    to_remove = []\n    for el in p_el.iter():\n        if el is p_el:\n            continue\n        if localname(el.tag) in remove_names:\n            to_remove.append(el)\n\n    for el in to_remove:\n        parent = parent_map.get(el)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(el)\n\n\ndef consolidate_text_nodes(p_el: ET.Element, ns: dict[str, str]) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    \"\"\"Return list of <hp:t> elements under paragraph, in document order.\"\"\"\n    return list(p_el.findall(\".//hp:t\", ns))\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text(p_el: ET.Element, ns: dict[str, str], new_text: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Set paragraph text by consolidating all <hp:t> nodes into the first one.\"\"\"\n    t_nodes = consolidate_text_nodes(p_el, ns)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return\n\n    parent_map = {child: parent for parent in p_el.iter() for child in parent}\n\n    t_nodes[0].text = new_text\n\n    #
